An Exploratory Cost‐Utility Analysis of Screening and Treating Immigrants in Canada for Hepatitis D

Authors: Jiahao Zhao, Diedron Lewis, John‐Michael Gamble, Zahid A. Butt, William W. L. Wong
Original Abstract
ABSTRACT Hepatitis D is common among immigrants in Canada, who account for approximately 23% of the population and tend to originate from high‐endemic countries. Mortality rates are notably high among those with chronic infections, and immigrants are less likely to engage in screening and treatment services. This paper aims to assess the cost‐utility of screening and treating Hepatitis D Virus (HDV)‐positive immigrants in Canada compared with a scenario in which no screening is conducted. This analysis takes a public‐payer perspective, uses a lifetime time horizon with a 1.5% annual discount rate, and expresses costs in 2023 Canadian dollars. A state‐transition model representing the natural progression of chronic hepatitis B and hepatitis D was developed in TreeAge Pro and mapped onto three interventions: screening and treatment with bulevirtide; screening and treatment with pegylated interferon alfa‐2a; and screening and treatment with both bulevirtide and pegylated interferon alfa‐2a. The analysis was conducted in accordance with the guidelines of Canada's Drug Agency. The incremental cost‐effectiveness ratio (ICER) was estimated for each intervention. Model sensitivity was performed using both univariate and probabilistic sensitivity analyses. All screening and treatment strategies resulted in a reduction in cases of decompensated cirrhosis, hepatocellular carcinoma and liver death, with combination therapy demonstrating the most favourable outcomes. Nevertheless, HDV screening and treatment with pegylated interferon alfa‐2a monotherapy was the only cost‐effective strategy (ICER: $23,177/QALY). While monotherapy with pegylated interferon alfa‐2a proved cost‐effective, combination therapy with bulevirtide is likely more clinically effective and may be cost‐effective if treatment costs decrease significantly.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
ABSTRACT Hepatitis D is common among immigrants in Canada, who account for approximately 23% of the population and tend to originate from high‐endemic countries.

恩替卡韦合成最新进展

Recent Progress in the Synthesis of Entecavir

Recent Progress in the Synthesis of Entecavir

Authors: PanPan Wang, Xiaolei Wang
Original Abstract
Entecavir, as the most effective hepatitis B virus (HBV) DNA polymerase inhibitor currently used in the clinical treatment of chronic hepatitis B, holds significant clinical value. This paper systematically reviews the latest research advances (2012–2025) in the total synthesis of entecavir. Based on the differences in synthetic strategies for constructing its core cyclopentane skeleton, existing synthetic routes are categorized into three types: Carbon ring construction strategies mediated by cycloaddition and ring‐closing metathesis, multi‐catalytic cascade and fully catalytic asymmetric synthesis strategies, and stereoselective epoxide transformation and tandem reaction strategies. This classification not only clearly presents the technical characteristics of different synthetic approaches but also provides an important reference for the development of carbocyclic nucleoside analogs.

非洲肝细胞癌:流行病学、预防和结果

Hepatocellular Carcinoma in Africa: Epidemiology, Prevention and Outcomes

Hepatocellular Carcinoma in Africa: Epidemiology, Prevention and Outcomes

Authors: Joseph A. Akambase, Ponsiano Ocama, Jose D. Debes
Original Abstract
Purpose of review: This review synthesizes the current knowledge of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) epidemiology across Northern and sub-Saharan Africa. We examine how causes of HCC differ by region, highlight prevention and early detection strategies, and identify gaps in treatment and palliative care to improve outcomes. Recent findings: The causes of HCC vary markedly across the continent. HCV predominates in Northern Africa, whereas HBV infection and dietary aflatoxin exposure prevail in sub-Saharan Africa. Rising obesity, diabetes, harmful alcohol use, and hepatitis D co-infection are important but under-researched contributors. Most patients still present with advanced disease, fewer than 6% receive curative therapy, median survival is measured in months, and access to palliative care remains dismal. Summary: HCC poses a major challenge in Africa, marked by high incidence, early age at diagnosis, and among the worst survival outcomes worldwide. Primary prevention through HBV birth-dose vaccination, aflatoxin mitigation, and population-level HCV treatment offers the most effective way to reduce mortality, yet implementation barriers persist.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

Cabotegravir 在艾滋病毒感染者中的真实安全性和有效性:日本上市后监测的中期报告。

Real-World Safety and Effectiveness of Cabotegravir in People Living with HIV: Interim Report of a Post-marketing Surveillance in Japan.
作者： Yoichiro Yoshikawa, Takako Nagao, Tomoko Matsukawa, Yuko Maeno, Akemi Sebata, Miwako Suzuki, Shiro Ibe, Akiko Fukuda, Dong Wang
摘要中文翻译Cabotegravir (CAB) 是一种整合酶链转移抑制剂,也是第一种针对人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV) 的长效注射型抗逆转录病毒药物,与利匹韦林 (rilpivirine) 一起给药。由于日本的批准主要依赖于海外和国际合作试验,因此国内现实世界的证据仍然有限。这项研究评估了 CAB 在日本艾滋病毒感染者 (PLHIV) 中的现实安全性和有效性。这项中期分析(2022 年 6 月 27 日和 2025 年 6 月 17 日)是参与 HIV 相关药物合作调查的机构对接受 CAB 治疗的日本 PLHIV 进行的上市后监测的一部分。通过药物不良反应(ADR)的发生率来评估安全性。使用 Fisher 精确检验和 χ2 检验检查 ADR 发生率与背景特征之间的关联,无需进行多重性调整。使用通过对数转换的 HIV RNA 拷贝数/mL 和外周 CD4+ 细胞计数评估的病毒学抑制来评估有效性。安全性和有效性分析组分别包括 121 名和 117 名参与者;大多数年龄在20岁以上,其中超过96%是男性参与者。总体而言,45 名参与者 (37.19%) 报告了 76 起 ADR。最常见的不良反应是注射部位疼痛和肌肉骨骼疼痛(各 12.40%)。报告医生未明确认为与 CAB 无关的四种严重 ADR 发生在 4 名参与者身上:乙型肝炎再激活、猴痘、梅毒和 2 型糖尿病。按参与者特征进行的分层分析显示,年龄 ≥ 30 岁至 < 40 岁(p = 0.038)、无过敏(p = 0.016)和有合并症(p = 0.045)的参与者中 ADR 发生率较高。在整个随访过程中,HIV RNA 拷贝仍然受到抑制,CD4+ 细胞计数也维持在与治疗开始时相当的水平。这项中期分析表明,CAB 疗法是安全的,没有新的安全信号,而且是有效的,持续的病毒学抑制和稳定的 CD4+ 细胞计数证明了这一点。这些发现与海外和国际合作临床研究报告的结果一致。对于人类免疫缺陷病毒 (PLHIV) 感染者来说,每天服用多种口服药物可能具有挑战性。卡博特韦 (Cabotegravir) 与利匹韦林 (rilpivirine) 联合给药,是第一种长效注射 HIV 治疗药物,可每月或每 2 个月注射一次。它在日本的批准主要基于海外和国际合作研究,日本的真实临床证据有限。为了弥补这一差距,我们进行了上市后监测,以确认卡博特韦对日本艾滋病毒感染者的安全性和有效性。这是一项为期 3 年的上市后监测分析,包括日本 10 家机构的 121 名参与者,这些参与者在改用卡博特韦之前已被病毒学抑制了 6 个多月。在随访期间,45名参与者经历了76种药物不良反应(37.19%),包括注射部位疼痛和肌肉骨骼疼痛(各12.40%)、口腔疱疹、高胆固醇、注射部位增厚或硬化、发烧和白细胞计数减少(各2.48%)。这些发现与之前的临床研究报告一致。年龄≥30岁至<40岁、无过敏以及患有其他疾病的参与者中观察到较高的药物不良反应发生率。大多数与卡博特韦相关的药物不良反应发生在治疗开始后 180 天内。在整个随访期间,HIV RNA 拷贝仍然受到抑制,CD4+ 细胞计数维持在与治疗开始时相似的水平。总体而言,这些结果证实了卡博特韦在日本的现实安全性和有效性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述研究了卡博特韦(一种长效注射整合酶链转移抑制剂与利匹韦林联合给药)在日本艾滋病毒感染者(PLHIV)中的真实安全性和有效性,重点关注上市后监测结果及其在国内环境中临床表现的核心问题。
证据基础与方法
该审查报告了对 2022 年 6 月 27 日至 2025 年 6 月 17 日在日本进行的持续上市后监测的 3 年中期分析,包括 10 个机构的 121 名参与者,他们在改用卡博特韦之前已被病毒学抑制了 6 个多月。该综述描述了通过药物不良反应 (ADR) 发生率以及使用 Fisher 精确检验和 χ2 检验与参与者特征的关联(无需多重调整)进行的安全性评估,以及通过病毒学抑制(HIV RNA 拷贝数/mL)和 CD4+ 细胞计数进行的有效性评估。
共识与结论
审查得出的结论是,卡博特韦疗法对于日本艾滋病病毒感染者是安全的,没有发现新的安全信号,并且在整个随访过程中持续的病毒学抑制和稳定的 CD4+ 细胞计数证明了卡博特韦疗法是有效的。最常见的不良反应是注射部位疼痛和肌肉骨骼疼痛(各 12.40%),严重的不良反应包括乙型肝炎再激活、猴痘、梅毒和 2 型糖尿病,但报告医生没有明确认为与卡博特韦相关。
争议与缺口
该审查强调了卡博特韦在日本国内的实际证据有限,因为其批准主要依赖于海外和国际合作试验。在年龄 30 岁至 <40 岁的参与者、无过敏者和有合并症的参与者中观察到较高的 ADR 发生率,但这些关联未针对多重性进行调整,需要进一步验证。该审查不会报告中期分析期之后的长期安全结果。
科研与实践启示
该审查意味着需要持续进行上市后监测,以确认卡博特韦对日本艾滋病毒感染者(尤其是 ADR 发生率较高的亚组)的长期安全性和有效性。它建议进一步研究特定人口和临床亚组中 ADR 模式的潜在机制,并使用调整后的统计方法验证观察到的关联。

Real-World Safety and Effectiveness of Cabotegravir in People Living with HIV: Interim Report of a Post-marketing Surveillance in Japan.

Authors: Yoichiro Yoshikawa, Takako Nagao, Tomoko Matsukawa, Yuko Maeno, Akemi Sebata, Miwako Suzuki, Shiro Ibe, Akiko Fukuda, Dong Wang
Original Abstract
Cabotegravir (CAB) is an integrase strand transfer inhibitor and the first long-acting injectable antiretroviral agent for human immunodeficiency virus (HIV), administered with rilpivirine. Because approval in Japan relied largely on overseas and international collaborative trials, domestic real-world evidence remains limited. This study evaluated the real-world safety and effectiveness of CAB in people living with HIV (PLHIV) in Japan. This interim analysis (June 27, 2022 and June 17, 2025) was part of an ongoing post-marketing surveillance of Japanese PLHIV treated with CAB at institutions participating in the HIV-related Drugs Cooperative Survey. Safety was assessed by the incidence of adverse drug reactions (ADRs). Associations between ADR incidence and background characteristics were examined using Fisher’s exact and χ2 tests without multiplicity adjustment. Effectiveness was evaluated using virologic suppression assessed by log-transformed HIV RNA copies/mL and peripheral CD4+ cell counts. A total of 121 and 117 participants were included in the safety and efficacy analysis sets, respectively; most were aged over 20 years, and over 96% were male participants. Overall, 76 ADRs were reported in 45 participants (37.19%). The most frequent ADRs were injection site pain and musculoskeletal pain (12.40% each). Four serious ADRs that were not explicitly deemed unrelated to CAB by the reporting physicians occurred in 4 participants: hepatitis B reactivation, monkeypox, syphilis, and type 2 diabetes mellitus. Stratification analyses by participant characteristics revealed high ADR incidences among participants aged ≥ 30 to < 40 years (p = 0.038), without allergy (p = 0.016), and with comorbidities (p = 0.045). HIV RNA copies remained suppressed and CD4+ cell counts were maintained at levels comparable to treatment initiation throughout follow-up. This interim analysis indicates that CAB therapy is safe, with no new safety signals, and effective, as demonstrated by sustained virological suppression and stable CD4+ cell counts. These findings are consistent with outcomes reported in overseas and international collaborative clinical studies. Taking multiple oral medications every day can be challenging for people living with human immunodeficiency virus (PLHIV). Cabotegravir, co-administered with rilpivirine, is the first long-acting injectable HIV treatment that can be given once monthly or every 2 months. Its approval in Japan was primarily based on overseas and international collaborative studies, and real-world clinical evidence in Japan has been limited. To address this gap, a post-marketing surveillance has been conducted to confirm the safety and effectiveness of cabotegravir in Japanese PLHIV. This was a 3-year interim analysis of post-marketing surveillance including 121 participants at 10 institutions in Japan who were virologically suppressed for over 6 months before switching to cabotegravir. During follow-up, 45 participants experienced 76 adverse drug reactions (37.19%) that included injection site pain and musculoskeletal pain (12.40% each), and oral herpes, high cholesterol, thickened or hardened injection site, fever, and decreased white blood cell counts (2.48% each). These findings were consistent with those reported in previous clinical studies. Higher adverse drug reaction rates were observed among participants aged ≥ 30 to < 40 years, those without allergy, and those with other medical conditions. Most adverse drug reactions considered related to cabotegravir occurred within 180 days after treatment initiation. Throughout the follow-up period, HIV RNA copies remained suppressed and CD4+ cell counts were maintained at levels similar to those at treatment initiation. Overall, these results confirm the real-world safety and effectiveness of cabotegravir in Japan.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines the real-world safety and effectiveness of cabotegravir, a long-acting injectable integrase strand transfer inhibitor co-administered with rilpivirine, in people living with HIV (PLHIV) in Japan, focusing on post-marketing surveillance outcomes and the central question of its clinical performance in domestic settings.
Evidence base and method
The review reports a 3-year interim analysis of an ongoing post-marketing surveillance conducted in Japan from June 27, 2022 to June 17, 2025, including 121 participants at 10 institutions who were virologically suppressed for over 6 months before switching to cabotegravir. The review describes safety assessment via adverse drug reaction (ADR) incidence and associations with participant characteristics using Fisher’s exact and χ2 tests without multiplicity adjustment, and effectiveness evaluation through virologic suppression (HIV RNA copies/mL) and CD4+ cell counts.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that cabotegravir therapy is safe in Japanese PLHIV, with no new safety signals identified, and effective, as evidenced by sustained virologic suppression and stable CD4+ cell counts throughout follow-up. The most frequent ADRs were injection site pain and musculoskeletal pain (12.40% each), and serious ADRs included hepatitis B reactivation, monkeypox, syphilis, and type 2 diabetes mellitus, none explicitly deemed related to cabotegravir by reporting physicians.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights limited domestic real-world evidence for cabotegravir in Japan, as its approval relied largely on overseas and international collaborative trials. Higher ADR incidences were observed among participants aged 30 to <40 years, those without allergy, and those with comorbidities, but these associations were not adjusted for multiplicity and require further validation. The review does not report long-term safety outcomes beyond the interim analysis period.
Research and practice implications
The review implies a need for continued post-marketing surveillance to confirm long-term safety and effectiveness of cabotegravir in Japanese PLHIV, particularly among subgroups with higher ADR incidence. It suggests further investigation into the mechanisms underlying ADR patterns in specific demographic and clinical subgroups, and validation of the observed associations using adjusted statistical methods.

澳大利亚初级保健诊所对慢性乙型肝炎患者的级联护理监测。

Monitoring of the Cascade of Care in Patients With Chronic HBV in Primary Care Clinics in Australia.
作者： Dina Moussa, Jack Wallace, Jacqui Richmond, Joseph S Doyle, Long Nguyen, Douglas Boyle, Mark A Stoové, Jason Asselin, Victoria Polkinghorne, Marvad Ahad
摘要中文翻译为了在澳大利亚消除乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV),需要从医院护理转向社区护理。本研究旨在描述初级保健患者慢性乙型肝炎的患病率以及级联护理的接受情况。这项前瞻性队列研究于 2020 年 1 月 7 日至 2023 年 6 月 30 日期间在澳大利亚维多利亚州 76 个城市和地区初级保健诊所进行。从全科医生 (GP) 诊所的电子病历 (EMR) 中提取匿名的社会人口统计、临床和实验室数据。对患有 HBV 的临床客户队列进行了描述性分析。在整个研究期间,共有 346,927 人参加了预约。其中,25,212 人有 HBV 相关检测记录。491 名 (0.14%) 个体有当前 HBV 感染的证据,定义为 HBV 表面抗原 (HBsAg) 阳性和/或 HBV DNA 阳性。研究期间有 469 人到诊所就诊,其中 239 人(51%)为女性。在 GP EMR 中,只有 59 名患者 (13%) 拥有至少一项 HBV DNA 检测和至少一项 ALT 检测的证据。14 名患者 (3%) 曾接受过诊所护士或全科医生的 HBV 管理检查记录,78 名患者 (17%) 接受过病毒性肝炎专家的检查记录。九十二 (20%) 正在接受乙型肝炎治疗。在接受治疗的人中,23 人(25%)有接受过专科护理的记录。我们的数据显示,初级保健机构中慢性乙型肝炎患者的级联护理仍存在显着差距,有证据表明参与乙型肝炎级联护理所有阶段的患者比例很低。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述调查了澳大利亚初级保健机构中的慢性乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染,重点关注慢性乙型肝炎患者的级联护理。核心问题涉及慢性乙型肝炎的患病率以及初级保健诊所内级联护理(检测、监测、治疗和专家联系)的采用。
证据基础与方法
该评价是一项前瞻性队列研究,于 2020 年 7 月 1 日至 2023 年 6 月 30 日期间在澳大利亚维多利亚州 76 个城市和地区初级保健诊所进行。匿名社会人口统计、临床和实验室数据是从全科电子病历 (EMR) 中提取的。对患有 HBV 的临床客户队列进行了描述性分析。
共识与结论
该审查报告称,346,927 名个体中有 491 名(0.14%)有当前 HBV 感染的证据(HBsAg 阳性和/或 HBV DNA 阳性)。在研究期间到诊所就诊的 469 名患者中,只有 59 名患者 (13%) 拥有至少一项 HBV DNA 检测和至少一项 ALT 检测的证据。14 名患者 (3%) 有诊所护士或全科医生对 HBV 管理进行审查的记录,78 名患者 (17%) 接受了病毒性肝炎专家的审查。92 人 (20%) 正在接受 HBV 治疗,其中 23 人 (25%) 有接受专科护理的记录。
争议与缺口
该审查强调了初级保健中慢性乙型肝炎护理级联方面的显着差距,包括检测率低(346,927 人中的 25,212 人接受了检测)、监测有限(13% 进行了 HBV DNA 和 ALT 检测)、不频繁的管理审查(3% 由临床工作人员进行,17% 由专家进行)以及治疗联系不理想(25% 的治疗患者与专科护理相关)。所提供的证据没有报告这些差距的原因或解决这些差距的策略。
科研与实践启示
该审查意味着需要采取有针对性的干预措施来改善初级保健机构的乙型肝炎检测、监测和管理。具体优先事项包括提高 HBV DNA 和 ALT 检测率、加强与治疗患者的专家联系,以及加强基于临床的 HBV 管理审查,以解决级联护理中已发现的差距。

Monitoring of the Cascade of Care in Patients With Chronic HBV in Primary Care Clinics in Australia.

Authors: Dina Moussa, Jack Wallace, Jacqui Richmond, Joseph S Doyle, Long Nguyen, Douglas Boyle, Mark A Stoové, Jason Asselin, Victoria Polkinghorne, Marvad Ahad
Original Abstract
To achieve hepatitis B virus (HBV) elimination in Australia, a shift from hospital to community-based care is needed. This study aimed to describe the prevalence of chronic HBV and uptake of the cascade of care in primary care patients. This prospective cohort study was conducted in 76 urban and regional primary care clinics across Victoria, Australia between 1/7/2020 and 30/6/2023. Anonymised socio-demographic, clinical and laboratory data from general practice (GP) clinics' electronic medical records (EMR) were extracted. Descriptive analysis of the cohort of clinic clients with HBV was performed. A total of 346,927 individuals attended appointments across the study period. Of these, 25,212 had records indicating HBV-related testing. 491 (0.14%) individuals had evidence of current HBV infection defined as HBV surface antigen (HBsAg) positive and/or HBV DNA positive. 469 attended the clinics during the study period, among whom 239 (51%) were female. In the GP EMR, only 59 (13%) had evidence of at least one HBV DNA test and at least one ALT test ordered. Fourteen patients (3%) had record of being reviewed for HBV management by the clinic nurse or GP and 78 (17%) reviewed by a viral hepatitis specialist. Ninety-two (20%) were on treatment for HBV. Of people receiving treatment, 23 (25%) had a record of linkage to specialist care. Our data show that significant gaps in the cascade of care remain for people with chronic HBV in primary care settings, with a low proportion of patients having evidence of participating in all stages of the HBV cascade of care.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection in primary care settings in Australia, focusing on the cascade of care for patients with chronic HBV. The central question addresses the prevalence of chronic HBV and the uptake of the cascade of care (testing, monitoring, treatment, and specialist linkage) within primary care clinics.
Evidence base and method
The review is a prospective cohort study conducted in 76 urban and regional primary care clinics across Victoria, Australia, between 1 July 2020 and 30 June 2023. Anonymised socio-demographic, clinical, and laboratory data were extracted from general practice electronic medical records (EMR). Descriptive analysis was performed on the cohort of clinic clients with HBV.
Consensus and conclusions
The review reports that 491 (0.14%) of 346,927 individuals had evidence of current HBV infection (HBsAg positive and/or HBV DNA positive). Among 469 patients attending clinics during the study period, only 59 (13%) had evidence of at least one HBV DNA test and at least one ALT test ordered. Fourteen patients (3%) had records of being reviewed for HBV management by the clinic nurse or GP, and 78 (17%) were reviewed by a viral hepatitis specialist. Ninety-two (20%) were on treatment for HBV, of whom 23 (25%) had a record of linkage to specialist care.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights significant gaps in the cascade of care for chronic HBV in primary care, including low rates of testing (25,212 of 346,927 individuals tested), limited monitoring (13% with HBV DNA and ALT tests), infrequent management reviews (3% by clinic staff, 17% by specialists), and suboptimal treatment linkage (25% of treated patients linked to specialist care). The supplied evidence does not report reasons for these gaps or strategies to address them.
Research and practice implications
The review implies a need for targeted interventions to improve HBV testing, monitoring, and management in primary care settings. Specific priorities include enhancing HBV DNA and ALT testing rates, increasing specialist linkage for treated patients, and strengthening clinic-based HBV management reviews to address the identified gaps in the cascade of care.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

健康运动重点关注各年龄段的乙型肝炎预防

Health campaign spotlights hepatitis B prevention across all ages
新闻简报China marked progress toward its 2030 goal of eliminating viral hepatitis as a public health threat with a national campaign spotlighting hepatitis B prevention across all ages. Held in Beijing on July 18, 2026, the event brought together public health officials and clinicians to review advances since universal infant vaccination began in 2005. Coverage for the three-dose series now reaches 99.6%, and the timely birth dose is administered to 95.6% of newborns, contributing to a sharp decline in chronic infection among children under five to 0.30%. Despite these gains, an estimated 75 million people aged 1 to 69 live with chronic hepatitis B in China, and about 5.86% of this age group remains affected. Experts emphasized maintaining high childhood vaccination rates while expanding adult immunization and called for reducing stigma and increasing screening to reach the undiagnosed.（中文翻译失败，本栏显示英文回退。）
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表于2026-07-19;活动日期为北京健康运动期间的周六(暗指 2026 年 7 月 18 日)。
地点与对象
中国,北京;人口重点关注1至69岁的个人,包括儿童和成人。
事件
控制和消除乙型肝炎的公共卫生运动,评估实现 2030 年消除目标的进展情况。据《中国日报》报道,这是一场官员和临床医生齐聚北京的媒体活动。
规模、影响与风险
An estimated 75 million people aged 1 to 69 are living with chronic HBV infection in China (roughly one-third of global cases). Prevalence among children under five is 0.30%, down from higher levels in the 1990s; prevalence among ages 1 to 69 is 5.86%. HBV is highly contagious and can progress to cirrhosis and liver cancer. A significant proportion of infected individuals are unaware of their status.
应对、状态与不确定性
2005年以来,全民免费接种婴儿疫苗,三剂接种覆盖率达到99.6%,出生及时接种覆盖率达到95.6%。2025-2030年行动计划旨在保持较高的儿童疫苗接种率并扩大成人免疫覆盖率。基层卫生院重点做好教育、筛查、疫苗接种提醒、后续护理等工作。不知道自己的乙肝病毒状况的成年人比例以及扩大成人免疫接种工作的有效性仍然存在不确定性。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Health campaign spotlights hepatitis B prevention across all ages

News Brief
China marked progress toward its 2030 goal of eliminating viral hepatitis as a public health threat with a national campaign spotlighting hepatitis B prevention across all ages. Held in Beijing on July 18, 2026, the event brought together public health officials and clinicians to review advances since universal infant vaccination began in 2005. Coverage for the three-dose series now reaches 99.6%, and the timely birth dose is administered to 95.6% of newborns, contributing to a sharp decline in chronic infection among children under five to 0.30%. Despite these gains, an estimated 75 million people aged 1 to 69 live with chronic hepatitis B in China, and about 5.86% of this age group remains affected. Experts emphasized maintaining high childhood vaccination rates while expanding adult immunization and called for reducing stigma and increasing screening to reach the undiagnosed.
View five news elements
Time
Published on 2026-07-19; event date referenced as Saturday (implied July 18, 2026) during a health campaign in Beijing.
Location and population
China, Beijing; population focus on individuals aged 1 to 69 years, including children and adults.
Event
Public health campaign to control and eliminate hepatitis B, assessing progress toward the 2030 elimination target. Reported by China Daily as a media event with officials and clinicians gathering in Beijing.
Scale, impact and risk
An estimated 75 million people aged 1 to 69 are living with chronic HBV infection in China (roughly one-third of global cases). Prevalence among children under five is 0.30%, down from higher levels in the 1990s; prevalence among ages 1 to 69 is 5.86%. HBV is highly contagious and can progress to cirrhosis and liver cancer. A significant proportion of infected individuals are unaware of their status.
Response, status and uncertainty
Universal free infant vaccination since 2005 has achieved 99.6% coverage for the three-dose series and 95.6% for the timely birth dose. The 2025–2030 action plan aims to maintain high childhood vaccination rates and expand adult immunization coverage. Grassroots health centers are emphasized for education, screening, vaccination reminders, and follow-up care. Uncertainty remains regarding the proportion of adults unaware of their HBV status and the effectiveness of expanded adult immunization efforts.

Published: 2026-07-20  |  Publisher: Newswise
B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

CT 测量的脂肪和肌肉变化可预测慢性乙型肝炎相关肝硬化的严重肝脏并发症新闻明智 - 新闻明智

CT-Measured Fat and Muscle Changes Predict Severe Liver Complications in Chronic Hepatitis B-Related Cirrhosis | Newswise - Newswise
新闻简报兰州大学第一医院研究人员在《肝胆通讯》上发表的一项研究表明,通过标准 CT 扫描测量的内脏脂肪和肌肉的变化可以预测慢性乙型肝炎相关肝硬化患者的严重肝脏并发症。研究表明,对于肝硬化早期患者来说,内脏脂肪的逐渐退化意味着出现并发症的风险更高。相比之下,持续性肌肉萎缩是那些已经经历过严重肝功能失代偿事件的人的主要警告标志。这些发现通过利用常规腹部 CT 扫描(已经成为肝硬化患者标准护理的一部分)来监测组织变化而无需额外的昂贵或侵入性测试,满足了重要的临床需求。该研究表明,早期检测这些组织变化可以让临床医生提供个性化的营养和代谢支持,从而减缓肝损伤并提高患者的长期生存机会。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该文章于2026-07-20发表。研究成果刊登于《Hepatobiliary Communications》期刊,DOI标注显示该研究在2026-07-20前已完成并定稿。
地点与对象
该研究由兰州大学第一医院的研究人员进行,受试者为慢性乙型肝炎相关肝硬化患者。
事件
研究人员发现,CT 测量的内脏脂肪和肌肉的变化可以预测慢性乙型肝炎相关肝硬化的严重肝脏并发症。研究结果发表在《肝胆通讯》杂志上。
规模、影响与风险
研究发现,早期肝硬化的内脏脂肪变性和晚期肝硬化的肌肉萎缩是严重肝脏并发症的重要预测因素。这些信息可以帮助临床医生提供早期、个性化的营养和代谢支持,以减缓肝损伤并提高生存机会。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究通过提供一种使用常规腹部 CT 扫描监测组织变化的方法来解决未满足的临床需求,这种扫描已经在肝硬化患者身上进行过。该方法不需要额外的昂贵或侵入性测试。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

CT-Measured Fat and Muscle Changes Predict Severe Liver Complications in Chronic Hepatitis B-Related Cirrhosis | Newswise - Newswise

News Brief
A study published in Hepatobiliary Communications by researchers from The First Hospital of Lanzhou University has revealed that changes in visceral fat and muscle, as measured by standard CT scans, can predict severe liver complications in patients with chronic hepatitis B-related cirrhosis. The research indicates that for patients in the early stages of cirrhosis, the gradual degeneration of visceral fat signals a higher risk of complications. In contrast, persistent muscle wasting serves as a primary warning marker for those who have already experienced severe hepatic decompensation events. These findings address a significant clinical need by utilizing routine abdominal CT scans, which are already part of standard care for cirrhosis patients, to monitor tissue changes without the need for additional expensive or invasive tests. The study suggests that early detection of these tissue changes can allow clinicians to provide personalized nutritional and metabolic support, thereby slowing liver damage and improving long-term survival chances for patients.
View five news elements
Time
The article was published on 2026-07-20. The study was published in Hepatobiliary Communications with a DOI reference indicating the research was conducted and finalized before 2026-07-20.
Location and population
The study was conducted by researchers from The First Hospital of Lanzhou University, involving patients with chronic hepatitis B-related cirrhosis.
Event
Researchers identified that CT-measured changes in visceral fat and muscle can predict severe liver complications in chronic hepatitis B-related cirrhosis. The findings were published in Hepatobiliary Communications.
Scale, impact and risk
The study found that visceral fat degeneration in early-stage cirrhosis and muscle wasting in advanced cirrhosis are significant predictors of severe liver complications. This information can help clinicians provide early, personalized nutritional and metabolic support to slow liver damage and improve survival chances.
Response, status and uncertainty
The research addresses an unmet clinical demand by providing a method to monitor tissue changes using routine abdominal CT scans, which are already performed on cirrhosis patients. This method does not require additional expensive or invasive testing.

尽管总体覆盖率很高,但世卫组织和联合国儿童基金会质疑印度新生儿乙型肝炎疫苗数据

WHO, UNICEF question India's newborn hepatitis B vaccine data despite strong overall coverage
新闻简报2026 年 7 月 15 日,世界卫生组织和联合国儿童基金会质疑印度对新生儿乙型肝炎疫苗接种覆盖率的官方估计,认为报告的 92% 覆盖率夸大了婴儿在分娩 24 小时内接受关键出生剂量的实际比例。这些机构估计全国实际覆盖率仅为70%,这表明近30%的新生儿错过了建议时间窗口内的疫苗接种。这种差异引起了人们对覆盖率分母和目标人群的担忧,因为疫苗对于预防慢性乙型肝炎至关重要,慢性乙型肝炎会显着增加以后患肝硬化和肝癌的风险。世卫组织和儿童基金会指出,他们了解印度正在进行的全国家庭健康调查(NFHS-6),并将在调查结果公布后对其进行审查。印度政府的评论尚未发表,《今日印度》已联系联邦卫生部寻求回应。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该事件于 2026 年 7 月 15 日进行了报道,基于该日发布的世界卫生组织-联合国儿童基金会国家免疫覆盖率估算 (WUENIC)。该数据涉及 2025 年乙型肝炎出生剂量覆盖率。
地点与对象
该活动涉及印度的新生儿,特别关注出生后 24 小时内接种的乙型肝炎出生剂量疫苗的覆盖范围。
事件
世卫组织和联合国儿童基金会质疑印度对新生儿乙型肝炎疫苗接种覆盖率的官方估计,称报告的数字夸大了在分娩 24 小时内接受关键出生剂量的婴儿的比例。印度政府报告的覆盖率高达 92%,而世界卫生组织和联合国儿童基金会估计全国实际覆盖率仅为 70%。[N3、N8、N9、N10、N11]。
规模、影响与风险
这一差异表明,每10名新生儿中近3名未能在推荐时间窗口内接种疫苗。慢性乙型肝炎在日后会增加罹患肝硬化和肝癌的风险,因此及时接种出生剂次疫苗至关重要。
应对、状态与不确定性
世卫组织和联合国儿童基金会了解印度正在进行的全国家庭健康调查(NFHS-6),并将在调查结果公布后对其进行审查。印度政府的评论尚未发表,《今日印度》已联系联邦卫生部寻求回应。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

WHO, UNICEF question India's newborn hepatitis B vaccine data despite strong overall coverage

News Brief
On July 15, 2026, WHO and UNICEF questioned India's official estimates on newborn hepatitis B vaccination coverage, suggesting that the reported 92% coverage overstates the actual proportion of babies receiving the critical birth dose within 24 hours of delivery. The agencies estimated the actual national coverage at only 70%, indicating that nearly 30% of newborns missed the vaccine within the recommended time window. This discrepancy raises concerns over coverage denominators and the target population, as the vaccine is crucial in preventing chronic hepatitis B, which significantly increases the risk of liver cirrhosis and liver cancer later in life. WHO and UNICEF noted that they are aware of India's ongoing National Family Health Survey (NFHS-6) and will review its findings once available. The Indian government's comments are pending as India Today has reached out to the Union health ministry for their response.
View five news elements
Time
The event was reported on July 15, 2026, based on the WHO-UNICEF Estimates of National Immunisation Coverage (WUENIC) released on that date. The data pertains to the hepatitis B birth dose coverage in 2025.
Location and population
The event involves newborns in India, specifically focusing on the coverage of the hepatitis B birth dose vaccine administered within 24 hours of birth.
Event
WHO and UNICEF have questioned India's official estimates on newborn hepatitis B vaccination coverage, stating that the reported figures overstate the proportion of babies receiving the critical birth dose within 24 hours of delivery. The Indian government reported 92% coverage, while WHO and UNICEF estimated actual national coverage at only 70%. [N3, N8, N9, N10, N11].
Scale, impact and risk
The discrepancy indicates that nearly three in every 10 newborns missed the vaccine within the recommended time window. Chronic hepatitis B raises the risk of liver cirrhosis and liver cancer later in life, making timely birth-dose vaccination crucial.
Response, status and uncertainty
WHO and UNICEF are aware of India's ongoing National Family Health Survey (NFHS-6) and will review its findings once available. The Indian government's comments are pending as India Today has reached out to the Union health ministry for their response.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

研究揭示了乙型肝炎病毒引起的肝脏炎症的机制

Study reveals mechanism underlying HBV-induced liver inflammation
新闻简报重庆医科大学的研究人员发现了慢性乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染与肝脏炎症之间的分子机制。他们的研究于 2026 年 7 月 17 日发表在《基因与疾病》杂志上,表明 HBV 复制会扰乱肝细胞中的线粒体呼吸,导致活性氧 (ROS) 积聚。这种氧化应激会激活抗氧化调节剂 Nrf2,然后刺激促炎细胞因子 IL-6 和 IL-8 的产生。这些细胞因子磷酸化并激活 Jak2/Stat3 信号通路,驱动 Saa1、S100a9、Icam1 和 Socs3 等炎症基因的转录。在 HBV 复制细胞系和 pAAV/HBV1.2 小鼠模型中进行的实验证实,用 Mito-TEMPO 或抗氧化剂 N-乙酰半胱氨酸清除 ROS 可减少 Jak2/Stat3 激活、细胞因子水平和肝脏炎症。这些发现将 ROS 依赖性 Jak2/Stat3 信号传导定位为潜在的治疗靶点,以防止炎症驱动的慢性乙型肝炎进展为肝硬化和肝癌。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026 年 7 月 17 日。事件日期:所提供的证据中未报告。
地点与对象
研究在重庆医科大学进行;样本包括 HBV 转基因小鼠和慢性 HBV 感染患者的肝组织。人口背景:全球慢性乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染。
事件
发表在《基因与疾病》上的一项同行评审研究发现,活性氧 (ROS) 依赖性信号级联能够激活 Jak2/Stat3 通路,从而在慢性 HBV 感染中引发肝脏炎症反应。该事件是研究人员报告的机械发现,而不是疾病爆发或指导文件。
规模、影响与风险
不适用于机械研究;没有病例数、死亡或暴露报告。该研究强调,慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染是导致肝硬化和肝细胞癌的全球肝脏发病的主要原因,但没有提供新的规模数据。
应对、状态与不确定性
本研究为初步机理研究;没有描述公共卫生应对行动。不确定性包括将小鼠和细胞系的研究结果转化为人类治疗,以及在小鼠模型中缺乏抗氧化剂 NAC 以外的临床试验或干预措施的报道。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Study reveals mechanism underlying HBV-induced liver inflammation

News Brief
Researchers at Chongqing Medical University have uncovered a molecular mechanism linking chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection to liver inflammation. Their study, published in Genes & Diseases on 17 July 2026, shows that HBV replication disrupts mitochondrial respiration in hepatocytes, causing a buildup of reactive oxygen species (ROS). This oxidative stress activates the antioxidant regulator Nrf2, which then stimulates production of pro-inflammatory cytokines IL-6 and IL-8. These cytokines phosphorylate and activate the Jak2/Stat3 signaling pathway, driving transcription of inflammatory genes such as Saa1, S100a9, Icam1, and Socs3. Experiments in HBV-replicating cell lines and a pAAV/HBV1.2 mouse model confirmed that scavenging ROS with Mito-TEMPO or the antioxidant N-acetylcysteine reduced Jak2/Stat3 activation, cytokine levels, and liver inflammation. The findings position ROS-dependent Jak2/Stat3 signaling as a potential therapeutic target to prevent inflammation-driven progression of chronic hepatitis B to cirrhosis and liver cancer.
View five news elements
Time
Publication date: 17 July 2026. Event date: Not reported in the supplied evidence.
Location and population
Research conducted at Chongqing Medical University; samples included HBV transgenic mice and liver tissue from patients with chronic HBV infection. Population context: chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection globally.
Event
A peer-reviewed study published in Genes & Diseases identified a reactive oxygen species (ROS)-dependent signaling cascade that activates the Jak2/Stat3 pathway, triggering hepatic inflammatory responses in chronic HBV infection. The event is a mechanistic discovery reported by researchers, not a disease outbreak or guidance document.
Scale, impact and risk
Not applicable to a mechanistic study; no case counts, deaths, or exposures reported. The study highlights that chronic HBV infection is a major cause of global liver morbidity leading to cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma, but no new scale data are provided.
Response, status and uncertainty
The study is preliminary mechanistic research; no public-health response actions are described. Uncertainties include the translation of mouse and cell-line findings to human therapy and the absence of reported clinical trials or interventions beyond antioxidant NAC in mouse models.
原文 / Source
Published: 2026-07-16  |  Publisher: The Pharmaceutical Journal

验尸官对缺乏关于乙型肝炎再激活处方的国家指导表示担忧 - The Pharmaceutical Journal

Coroner raises concerns over lack of national guidance on prescribing for hepatitis B reactivation - The Pharmaceutical Journal
新闻简报在 41 岁患者 Tung Tran 死亡后,英国验尸官对缺乏关于乙型肝炎重新激活的处方和监测的国家指导表示严重关切。Tran于2013年接受了肾移植,并被诊断出患有慢性乙型肝炎,由于无意中停止了必要的治疗,导致乙型肝炎重新激活而引起的并发症于2025年9月12日去世。验尸官于 2026 年 7 月 10 日发布的报告强调,药物供应在 2025 年初改为送货上门,但由于假设肝病服务将继续开处方而停止。2025 年 8 月,Tran 因出现急性肝病症状而被转移到皇家自由医院,但由于身体状况太差,无法考虑进行肝移植。验尸官向英国肝脏研究协会 (BASL) 的英国病毒性肝炎小组和英国健康安全局提出了担忧,指出缺乏专门的委托来维持与服务的接触。BASL 回应称,他们并不主导临床指南的制定,而且 NHS 信托机构之间的责任也有所不同。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该事件发生于2025年9月12日,验尸官的报告于2026年7月10日发表,文章于2026年7月16日发表。
地点与对象
该事件涉及一名41岁的患者Tung Tran,他于2013年接受了肾移植,被诊断患有慢性乙型肝炎。该事件发生在英国,具体涉及皇家自由医院和内伦敦北验尸官管辖范围。
事件
一名验尸官对患者 Tung Tran 死亡后缺乏关于乙型肝炎再激活处方和监测的国家指导表示担忧,该患者因无意中止必要的医疗而死于乙型肝炎再激活并发症。
规模、影响与风险
该事件导致一人死亡。验尸官强调,由于缺乏预防乙型肝炎再激活的国家指导和专门委托,未来存在死亡风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
验尸官就缺乏全国性指导与专业化委托事宜向英国病毒性肝炎集团(BASL)及英国卫生安全局(UKHSA)表达关切。BASL回应称,该集团不负责临床指南的制定,且监测与处方责任在NHS各信托机构间存在差异。[N9,N11,N12,N13]。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Coroner raises concerns over lack of national guidance on prescribing for hepatitis B reactivation - The Pharmaceutical Journal

News Brief
A coroner in the UK has raised significant concerns over the lack of national guidance on prescribing and monitoring for hepatitis B reactivation, following the death of a 41-year-old patient, Tung Tran. Tran, who had a renal transplant in 2013 and was diagnosed with chronic hepatitis B, died on 12 September 2025 from complications arising from the reactivation of hepatitis B due to the inadvertent discontinuation of necessary medical treatment. The coroner's report, published on 10 July 2026, highlighted that the supply of medication changed to home delivery in early 2025 but was discontinued under the presumption that hepatology services would continue prescribing. Tran was transferred to the Royal Free Hospital in August 2025 with signs of acute liver disease but was too unwell to be considered for a liver transplant. The coroner directed concerns to the British Viral Hepatitis Group at the British Association for the Study of the Liver (BASL) and the UK Health Security Agency, noting a lack of specialized commissioning to maintain engagement with services. BASL responded that they do not lead the development of clinical guidelines and that responsibilities vary between NHS trusts.
View five news elements
Time
The event occurred on 12 September 2025, with the coroner's report published on 10 July 2026 and the article published on 16 July 2026.
Location and population
The event involved a 41-year-old patient, Tung Tran, who had a renal transplant in 2013 and was diagnosed with chronic hepatitis B. The incident occurred in the UK, specifically involving the Royal Free Hospital and the Inner London North coroner's jurisdiction.
Event
A coroner raised concerns over the lack of national guidance on prescribing and monitoring for hepatitis B reactivation following the death of a patient, Tung Tran, who died from complications of hepatitis B reactivation due to the inadvertent discontinuation of necessary medical treatment.
Scale, impact and risk
The incident resulted in one death. The coroner highlighted the risk of future deaths due to the lack of national guidance and specialized commissioning for hepatitis B reactivation prevention.
Response, status and uncertainty
The coroner directed concerns to the British Viral Hepatitis Group at BASL and the UK Health Security Agency regarding the lack of national guidance and specialized commissioning. BASL responded that they do not lead the development of clinical guidelines and that monitoring and prescribing responsibilities vary between NHS trusts. [N9, N11, N12, N13].

世卫组织加强土库曼斯坦医疗专业人员应对乙型病毒性肝炎的能力 - Turkmenportal.com

WHO strengthens medical professionals’ capacity in Turkmenistan on viral hepatitis B - Turkmenportal.com
新闻简报世界卫生组织驻土库曼斯坦国家办事处于2025年3月12日至13日举办了“根据世界卫生组织建议和国家方案诊断和治疗乙型病毒性肝炎”培训课程。该课程由世界卫生组织/欧洲顾问Alexander Nersesov教授主持,旨在提高土库曼斯坦医务人员抗击病毒性肝炎的能力。培训在传染病治疗和预防中心以视频会议方式进行,涵盖了基本知识有关乙型肝炎及其诊断、分类和治疗的信息,包括针对慢性病例、妊娠和特殊患者群体以及丁型肝炎的具体指导。参与者包括专门从事病毒性肝炎诊断和治疗的医生、初级卫生保健人员、传染病专家以及以 Myrat Karryev 命名的国立医科大学传染病系的代表。该倡议预计将有助于改善患者的治疗效果,并加强土库曼斯坦控制和管理病毒性肝炎的努力。
查看新闻五要素
时间
据2025年3月18日报道[N1],培训班于2025年3月12日至13日举行。
地点与对象
该活动在土库曼斯坦举行,特别是在传染病治疗预防中心,涉及来自各个医疗机构的医疗专业人员,包括传染病中心理事会、初级卫生保健提供者和以迈拉特·卡里耶夫 (Myrat Karryev) 命名的国立医科大学 [N2、N3、N4]。
事件
世界卫生组织(WHO)驻土库曼斯坦国家办事处举办了“根据世界卫生组织建议和国家方案诊断和治疗乙型病毒性肝炎”的培训课程。该课程由世卫组织/欧洲顾问 Alexander Nersesov 教授主持,涵盖了乙型肝炎及其诊断、分类和治疗的基本信息,包括针对慢性病例、妊娠和特殊患者群体以及丁型肝炎 [N1、N2、N5、N6] 的具体指导。
规模、影响与风险
此次培训旨在提高土库曼斯坦医疗专业人员抗击病毒性乙型肝炎的能力。参加人员包括专门从事病毒性肝炎诊断和治疗的医生、初级卫生保健人员、传染病专家以及国立医科大学传染病科代表[N4]。该举措预计将有助于改善患者的治疗效果,并加强土库曼斯坦控制和管理病毒性肝炎的努力[N7]。
应对、状态与不确定性
培训以视频会议模式进行,涵盖乙型病毒性肝炎诊断和治疗的最新知识和最佳实践,符合世卫组织指南和国家方案。知名媒体土库曼门户网报道了该活动[N3,N6]。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

WHO strengthens medical professionals’ capacity in Turkmenistan on viral hepatitis B - Turkmenportal.com

News Brief
The World Health Organization (WHO) Country Office in Turkmenistan held a training course on the "Diagnostics and treatment of viral hepatitis B according to WHO recommendations and national protocol" from March 12 to 13, 2025. Led by WHO/Europe consultant Professor Alexander Nersesov, the course aimed to enhance the capacity of Turkmen medical professionals in combating viral hepatitis B. The training, conducted at the Infectious disease treatment-prevention center in videoconference mode, covered essential information on hepatitis B, its diagnosis, classification, and treatment, including specific guidance for chronic cases, pregnancy, and special patient groups, as well as hepatitis D. Participants included doctors specializing in the diagnosis and treatment of viral hepatitis, primary health care providers, infectious diseases specialists, and representatives from the department of infectious diseases at the State Medical University named after Myrat Karryev. The initiative is expected to contribute to improved patient outcomes and strengthen Turkmenistan's efforts to control and manage viral hepatitis.
View five news elements
Time
The training course was held from March 12 to 13, 2025, as reported on March 18, 2025 [N1].
Location and population
The event took place in Turkmenistan, specifically at the Infectious disease treatment-prevention center, involving medical professionals from various healthcare institutions, including the Directorate of Infectious Diseases Centers, primary health care providers, and the State Medical University named after Myrat Karryev [N2, N3, N4].
Event
The World Health Organization (WHO) Country Office in Turkmenistan conducted a training course on the "Diagnostics and treatment of viral hepatitis B according to WHO recommendations and national protocol". The course was led by WHO/Europe consultant, Professor Alexander Nersesov, and covered essential information on hepatitis B, its diagnosis, classification, and treatment, including specific guidance for chronic cases, pregnancy, and special patient groups, as well as hepatitis D [N1, N2, N5, N6].
Scale, impact and risk
The training aimed to enhance the capacity of Turkmen medical professionals in combating viral hepatitis B. The participants included doctors specializing in the diagnosis and treatment of viral hepatitis, primary health care providers, infectious diseases specialists, and representatives from the department of infectious diseases at the State Medical University [N4]. The initiative is expected to contribute to improved patient outcomes and strengthen Turkmenistan's efforts to control and manage viral hepatitis [N7].
Response, status and uncertainty
The training was conducted in videoconference mode and covered updated knowledge and best practices in viral hepatitis B diagnosis and treatment, aligning with both WHO guidelines and national protocols. The event was reported by Turkmenportal.com, a reputable media source [N3, N6].
